country_event = {

	id = MGU_event.8
	title = MGU_event.8.t
	desc = MGU_event.8.d
	picture = GFX_event_MGU_iroha_news
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	option = {
		name = MGU_event.8.a
		MGR = {
			MGU_Tamaki_Iroha = {
				set_nationality = MGU
			}
			MGU_Mitsuki_Felicia = {
				set_nationality = MGU
			}
			MGU_Futaba_Sana = {
				set_nationality = MGU
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 100
		army_experience = 100
	}
}

country_event = {
	#大选的结果
	id = MGU_event.18
	title = MGU_event.18.t
	desc = MGU_event.18.desc
	picture = GFX_event_MGU_iroha_news
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	immediate = {
	}
	option = {
		#红晴结菜
		name = MGU_event.18.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = MGU_country_flag_yuna_heartbrokened
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = "MGU_Kureha_Yuna"
			country_leader = {
				ideology = despotism
				#traits = { }
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				id = -1
				traits = {
					MGU_Alliance_Leader_1
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			#	load_focus_tree = MGU_brazil_tree_focus
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		#七海八千代
		name = MGU_event.18.b
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = democratic
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			#	load_focus_tree = MGU_brazil_tree_focus
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		#梓美冬
		name = MGU_event.19.c
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = democratic
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = "MGU_Azusa_Mifuyu"
			country_leader = {
				ideology = socialism
				#traits = { }
				expire = "1999.1.1.1"
				id = -1
				traits = {
					MGU_Alliance_Leader_4
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			#	load_focus_tree = MGU_brazil_tree_focus
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		#下一页 禁用
		name = MGU_event.18.e
		country_event = MGU_event.19
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	#私密会面
	id = magicunion.721
	title = magicunion.721.t
	desc = magicunion.721.d
	picture = GFX_event_MGU_madohomu
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			add_command_power = 9000
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = magicunion.721.a
		every_unit_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_character = MGU_Akemi_Homura
			}
			remove_unit_leader_trait = chaos_mahoushojo
			add_unit_leader_trait = order_mahoushojo
			add_attack = 1
			add_defense = 1
			add_planning = 1
			add_logistics = 1
		}
	}
}

    		name = magicunion.1065_MGU.b
            country_event = { id = MGU_event.18 days = 30 }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    is_faction_leader = yes
                }
                MGU = { dismantle_faction = yes }
            }
            if = {
                limit = {
                is_faction_leader = no
                is_in_faction = yes
                }
                MGU = { leave_faction = yes }
            }
                set_party_name = { ideology = fascism long_name = "magius之翼" name = "magius之翼" }
                set_party_name = { ideology = democratic long_name = "魔联-自由派" name = "魔联-自由派" }
                set_party_name = { ideology = communism long_name = "魔联-温和派" name = "魔联-温和派" }
                set_party_name = { ideology = neutrality long_name = "魔联-建制派" name = "魔联-建制派" }
            hidden_effect = {
                add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = 1.0 }
                add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.5 }
                add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.33 }
                add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.25 }
            }
		    ai_chance = {
			    base = 100
		    }
    		trigger = {
    			tag = MGU
            }
    	}

}

country_event = {
	##魔女之夜的降临 POW！！
	id = magicunion.10672_MGU
	title = magicunion.10672_MGU.t
	desc = magicunion.10672_MGU.desc
	picture = GFX_event_MGU_envelope
	major = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = MS1
				country_exists = MGU
				MS1 = {
					has_deployed_air_force_size = {
						size < 1000
					}
				}
			}
			MS1 = {
				add_manpower = 3000
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = strike_witches_equipment
					amount = 300
					producer = MS1
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = MS1
				country_exists = MGU
				MS1 = {
					has_army_manpower = {
						size < 1000000
					}
				}
			}
			MS1 = {
				add_manpower = 200000
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = magic_assault_equipment
					amount = 5000
					producer = MS1
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 3000
					producer = MS1
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = magic_artillery_equipment
					amount = 2500
					producer = MS1
				}
				1164 = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"魔女之夜的使魔\" division_template = \"魔女之夜的使魔\" start_experience_factor = 0.6 start_equipment_factor = 1 "
						owner = MS1
						count = 30
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_idea = MGU_new_age_army_Walpurgis_Night
					}
					country_event = {
						id = magicunion.10672
						days = 180
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = MS1
				country_exists = MGU
				MS1 = {
					has_army_manpower = {
						size > 1000000
					}
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = magicunion.10672
				days = 30
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = magicunion.1067_MGU.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		trigger = {
			tag = MGU
		}
	}
}

	# 南美局势-结束战争？ 和谈
	id = magicunion.1110
	title = magicunion.1110.t
	desc = magicunion.1110.desc
	picture = GFX_event_MGU_envelope
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	option = {
		#只能接受
		name = magicunion.1110.a
		MGU = {
			white_peace = {
				tag = PAN
				message = MGU_PAN_war_peace
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_enemy_country = {
				limit = {
					is_valid_peace_target = yes
					capital_scope = {
						is_on_continent = south_america
					}
				}
				white_peace = {
					tag = PAN
					#	message = MGU_PAN_war_peace
				}
			}
		}
		remove_from_faction = CHL
		remove_from_faction = PA1
		MGU = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					LHS = {
						has_capitulated = yes
					}
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = LHS
					autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					PA1 = {
						has_capitulated = yes
					}
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = PA1
					autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		#战争 继续
		name = magicunion.1110.b
		MGU = {
			country_event = generic.2009
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	##晓美焰疲劳值恢复
	id = magicunion.1189
	title = magicunion.1189.t
	desc = magicunion.1189.desc
	picture = GFX_event_MGU_envelope
	major = no
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired > 0
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				global.MGU_homura_tired = -1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired > 100
				}
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired < 134
				}
			}
			add_ideas = MGU_Need_rest
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_II
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_III
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_IV
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired > 133
				}
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired < 167
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest
			add_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_II
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_III
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_IV
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired > 166
				}
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired < 201
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_II
			add_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_III
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_IV
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired > 200
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_II
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_III
			add_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_IV
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					global.MGU_homura_tired < 101
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_II
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_III
			remove_ideas = MGU_Need_rest_IV
		}
		country_event = {
			days = 3
			id = magicunion.1189
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = magicunion.1189.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
		trigger = {
			tag = MGU
		}
	}
}

	title = MTK.111.t
	desc = MTK.111.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MTK.111.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				MGU = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = ROOT
						value > 79
					}
				}
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				fighting_army_strength_ratio = {
					tag = FROM
					ratio < 0.7
				}
				add = 20
			}
		}
		MGU = { add_to_faction = PAN }
		MGU = { add_to_faction = PAS }
		MGU = { add_to_faction = CUB }
		MGU = { add_to_faction = PAJ }
	}
	option = {
		name = MTK.110.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
		}
		MGU = { country_event = MTK.112 }
	}	
}

news_event = {
	id = MTK_news.2
	title = MTK_news.2.t
	desc = MTK_news.2.d
	picture = [MTK_LHS_super_event]
	major = yes
	trigger = {
		wants_world_news = yes
	}	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MTK_news.2.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				tag = MGU
				tag = LHS
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { adorable_heart_super_event_var = 118 }			
			adorable_heart_fire_super_event = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = MTK_news.2.b
		trigger = {
			has_government = communism
			NOT = {
				tag = MGU
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = LHS
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { adorable_heart_super_event_var = 118 }			
			adorable_heart_fire_super_event = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = MTK_news.2.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { adorable_heart_super_event_var = 118 }		
			adorable_heart_fire_super_event = yes
		}
	}
}

news_event = {
	id = MTK_news.6
	title = MTK_news.6.t
	desc = MTK_news.6.d
	picture = GFX_MTK_super_news_6
	major = yes
	trigger = {
		wants_world_news = yes
	}	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MTK_news.6.a
		trigger = {
			tag = MGU
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_government = communism
					NOT = {
						tag = MGU
					}
				}
				country_event = MTK.104
			}
		}
		501 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					industrial_complex > 0
				}
				damage_building = {
					type = industrial_complex
					damage = 2.5
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					arms_factory > 0
				}
				damage_building = {
					type = arms_factory
					damage = 2.5
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					infrastructure > 0
				}
				damage_building = {
					type = infrastructure
					damage = 2.5
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = MTK_news.6.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				tag = MGU
			}
			has_government = communism
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = MTK_news.6.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = yuki.1.d
	picture = yuki.1.p
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = yuki.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			retire_character = MGU_Aika_Himena
			set_country_flag = MGU_yuki_flag
			retire_character = MGU_Hiro
			retire_character = MGU_Ema
		}
		add_manpower = -10
		custom_effect_tooltip = yuki_1_a_name
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = MGU_yuki_enter
			LFC = {
				set_country_flag = MGU_yuki_enter
			}
			news_event = {
				id = yuki.2
			}
			country_event = {
				id = yuki.3
				days = 5
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = yuki.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = yuki_1_b_name
		hidden_effect = {
			complete_national_focus = MGU_Next
			set_country_flag = MGU_yuki_stop
		}
	}
}

#内战爆发
country_event = {
	id = yuki.16
	title = yuki.16.t
	desc = yuki.16.d
	picture = yuki.16.p
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = yuki.16.a
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = MGU_we_win_1
						check_variable = {
							MGU_land_1_value_left_number > MGU_land_1_value_right_number
						}
						check_variable = {
							MGU_land_1_value_left_number = MGU_land_1_value_right_number
						}
					}
				}
				MRB = {
					transfer_state = 1164
					add_state_core = 1164
					transfer_state = 502
					add_state_core = 502
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = MGU_dem_win_1
						check_variable = {
							MGU_land_1_value_left_number < MGU_land_1_value_right_number
						}
					}
				}
				MST = {
					transfer_state = 1164
					add_state_core = 1164
					transfer_state = 502
					add_state_core = 502
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = MGU_we_win_2
						check_variable = {
							MGU_land_2_value_left_number > MGU_land_2_value_right_number
						}
						check_variable = {
							MGU_land_2_value_left_number = MGU_land_2_value_right_number
						}
